# Statistical relationship inventory — pass 1 main and plan shard

Runtime agent: root/statistics_one_main. Model: gpt-6.1-sol. Reasoning effort: high. Start mode: FRESH_SPAWN. Assigned scope: all 89 DOC-001 local MS records, including every Table 2/3 time row, all Figure 2 panels, both Figure 3 time panels for each of 11 outcomes, all 11 Figure 4 contrasts, narrative matches and statistical definitions; and 29 result-relevant shared DOC-002/DOC-003 design families. Total 118 S records, S001–S118. Both time panels remain separately described inside each Figure 3 outcome record. No supplement-only result relationship is assigned here; DOC-004 eTable 9 is cited only as the report comparator for the planned one-year weight/waist analysis families.

All records below have PASS_1_COMPLETE. Pass 2 must revisit every individual ID in a different fresh runtime agent and add PASS_2_COMPLETE. No pass-2 status is asserted here.

## Shared inference definitions and checking rules

These definitions are part of each applicable record, rather than unstated assumptions. I = energy-reduced Mediterranean diet plus physical activity/behavioral support; C = unrestricted-energy Mediterranean diet. Main nutritional population I3272/C3311 after baseline FFQ/energy exclusions; multiple imputation for principal follow-up results. Table 2 p.5 and Table 3 pp.7–8 follow-up group columns are mean(SD); between-group values are mixed-model differences in baseline-to-6/12-month change, I minus C, with 95% CI and P. M p.4 states site/participant/family random intercepts, two-sided tests, P<.05, secondary analyses exploratory without multiplicity adjustment. Footnotes state site/couple random factors. All displayed Table 2/3 estimates are inside ordered intervals; all between-arm directions match the named contrast and applicable narrative. Rounding intervals use half of the last displayed unit. Modeled contrasts are not required to equal crude subtraction of displayed means.

Exact test statistic, specific test family, degrees of freedom, standard errors, covariance, MI pooling formula and variance estimator are not supplied for these main contrasts. Therefore exact numerical CI/P reconstruction is unavailable; a normal calculation is diagnostic only, never the identified test. The printed alcohol 12-month CI includes zero while P=.01, but the source does not identify the test/CI construction needed for a mechanically established compatibility claim; see excluded table. Six-month upper endpoint 0 can represent a small negative value after rounding. Other intervals and P values show no source-established incompatibility. No noninferiority/equivalence margin or claim is supplied.

Figure 2 and Figure 3 boxes are medians/IQR with 1.5-IQR whiskers, not confidence intervals. Figure 3 has separate 6- and 12-month columns in baseline-SD units. Figure 4 supplies paired arm proportions with 95% CIs and P, raw-unit observed boxplots, and threshold labels. All 22 Figure 4 proportion point estimates are contained in ordered arm intervals; percentage direction and P significance agree with the narrative (LDL P=.36 is not a favorable significant contrast). Figure 4 does not supply exact numerators, denominators, proportion-test family, estimator, or variance/correlation handling. Do not use denominators from nutritional tables or reverse-engineer exact counts. Axis '(95% CI)' wording is resolved by the detailed boxplot caption.

A shared design element across P and A is one relationship with all locations retained. These 29 families cover the result-relevant design union. Clinical endpoint definitions without current reported events are included in the hierarchy and measurement records; they are not paired to dietary results. Administrative, reference and operational-only content adds no inferential relationship.

## Pass-1 limits and handoff

All 118 assigned relationships completed; no scientific relationship-coverage gap. Exact CI/P reconstruction remains unavailable where sources omit test/CI construction, degrees of freedom, covariance, MI pooling or variance estimator. Unlabeled Figure2/3/4 graphical quantiles/frequencies are inspected as graphics. CV sample-size target power/formula and weight sample-size formula/inflation are absent; normal approximations are diagnostic. No external literature, web, raw-data audit, old candidate/report conclusions, custom Python or GPU. Reused layouts/maps/source-matched renders preserved; targeted direct Poppler26.01.0 extraction to stdout confirmed source passages. No new derivative needed.
# Statistical relationship inventory — pass 1 support A

Status: PASS_1_COMPLETE. Fresh statistical reviewer: /root/statistics_one_a, fixed model gpt-6.1-sol, high effort. Agent ID recorded in the coordinator manifest: root/statistics_one_a.

Exact disjoint scope: DOC-004 REPORT eTable 1 score definitions PDF p. 2; eTables 2–5 pp. 3–15, including continuation/model footnotes and the p. 10 score-specific sample footnote; eFigures 1–2 pp. 25–26. DOC-001 REPORT Methods p. 4, Table 2 p. 5, related narrative pp. 5–8 are matched comparators/context, not a second ownership of the main-paper statistical relationships. No plan-design or eTables 6–9/eFigure 3 relationships are assigned here.

81 stable S IDs, S201–S281: 61 variable/population families with 122 separate 6-/12-month change comparisons, four score-definition families, five common method/context families, four eFigure 1 panels retaining all three visits, seven eFigure 2 proportion comparisons. Every table family preserves its baseline, both arms' change estimates and confidence intervals, between-group estimate/interval/P, exact unit/population/time and footnotes in the complete evidence appendix below. The compact table does not substitute for that transcription. Every family was read and checked at both visits; no time-cell sampling occurred.

## Evidence and check rules

Sources and pre-existing assets are preserved. Reused native layout and complete fresh semantic mapping are the locators. Retained source-matched pp. 25/26 renders were inspected in full. Native/layout transcription of main pp. 4–6 was locally regenerated with direct CPU Poppler pdftotext 26.01.0, command `pdftotext -layout -f 4 -l 6 jama_saynorea_2019_oi_190106_1635377898.43062.pdf .ai_paper_validation/review_1_6_1/preprocessing/st1-a-main-methods-p4-6.txt`. It supplies exact immediate narrative/method context. No new OCR, custom Python, GPU, web, or earlier scientific conclusions were used.

For every table family: check both arm point estimates and between-arm estimate lie within their own printed 95% CI, lower endpoint does not exceed upper endpoint, signs and I-minus-C direction reconcile with the row, and significance versus CI exclusion of zero is compatible after allowing every displayed input its half-unit rounding interval. These checks found no non-reconciling table cell in this assigned scope. A displayed SD/IQR is not treated as a standard error/CI. Difference estimates are modeled, not recomputed as crude exact subtractions. Main p. 4 establishes mixed-effects linear models, site/participant/family random intercepts, two-sided tests, P<.05, exploratory secondary analyses and no additional adjustment. Exact degrees of freedom, coefficient covariance, variance estimator, test statistic and standard errors are absent; no convention supplies them. Therefore numerical inversion of rounded CI to a unique P is unavailable and is not a finding. Rounded endpoint zero may represent a small nonzero endpoint. No NI/equivalence margin or stopping conclusion applies to these dietary displays.

In the table below, each eTable 2 family is MI, N=3272 I/3311 C; each eTable 3–5 family is completers, n=2862 I/2883 C at 6 mo and n=2833 I/2943 C at 12 mo, except the explicit er-MedDiet asterisk n=3030 I/3090 C at 6 mo and n=2971 I/3095 C at 12 mo. Food units are g/week. eTable 5 units are individually retained. Between columns use `estimate [lower,upper]; P`; the evidence appendix preserves every corresponding arm estimate/CI and baseline value. `Contained; ordered; direction compatible` applies to all three estimates at each of both time points.

  0 points if the consumption was between 0–1 serving/wk; 1 point if the consumption was between 2–3 servings/wk and 2 points if the consumption was ≥4 servings/week.
d
  0 points if the consumption was ≥4 servings/week; 1 point if the consumption was between 2–3 servings/wk and 2 points if the consumption was between 0–1 serving/week.

 17-item er-MedDiet (0 to 17)*

 MDS (Trichopolou 0 to 9)
    Baseline, mean (SD)                         4.3 (1.7)              4.3 (1.6)
    6-month change                          0.2 (0.1 to 0.2)        -0.3 (-0.4 to -       0.5 (0.4 to 0.6)   <0.001
                                                                         0.3)
    12-month change                         0.2 (0.1 to 0.2)        -0.3 (-0.4 to -       0.5 (0.4 to 0.6)   <0.001
                                                                         0.3)
 MEDAS (0 to 14)
    Baseline, mean (SD)                         7.6 (1.9)              7.6 (1.9)
    6-month change                          2.5 (2.5 to 2.6)       1.4 (1.3 to 1.5)       1.1 (1.0 to 1.3)   <0.001
    12-month change                         2.7 (2.7 to 2.8)       1.6 (1.6 to 1.7)       1.1 (1.0 to 1.2)   <0.001
 PDQS (0 to 42)
    Baseline, mean (SD)                        21.0 (3.7)             21.0 (3.7)
    6-month change                          4.6 (4.5 to 4.8)       2.3 (2.1 to 2.4)       2.4 (2.2 to 2.6)   <0.001
    12-month change                         4.7 (4.5 to 4.8)       2.4 (2.3 to 2.5)       2.3 (2.1 to 2.5)   <0.001

Baseline data are means (SD). 6-month and 12-month change are means (95% CI) calculated using mixed effect
models incorporating site and intra-cluster correlations (couples) as random factors.
er-MedDiet, Energy reduced Mediterranean Diet; MDS, Mediterranean Diet Score, MEDAS, Mediterranean Diet
Adherence Screener; PDQS, Prime Diet Quality Score. The direction of all 4 food patterns is the same: a higher score
means a higher quality of the overall dietary pattern. The possible ranges were 0-17 for er-MedDiet, 0-9 for the MDS,
0-14 for MEDAS, and 0-42 for PDQS.

The minimum clinically important difference (MCID) can be considered 1 point for the MDS, because a 2 point
increment (roughly corresponding to one standard deviation) was associated in the fully adjusted model with a 25%
relative reduction in all-cause mortality (Trichopoulou et al. N Engl J Med 2003;348:2599-608, Table 4), coefficient
= log(0.75) = -0.2877. Therefore, 1 point in the MDS (corresponding to 0.5 SD) will lead to a 13% relative risk
reduction corresponding to a hazard ratio of 0.87, namely exp(-0.2877/2) = 0.87, which can be considered higher than
a minimal clinically significant effect from the subjective point of view of a patient.

For the MEDAS, in the PREDIMED trial, assessed as an observational study, and controlling for potential
confounding, 1-point increment was associated with a 10% reduction in the risk of the composite primary
cardiovascular end-point (multivariable-adjusted hazard ratio= 0.90, 95% CI, 0.85-0.96) and with a 6% reduction in
total mortality (multivariable-adjusted hazard ratio= 0.94, 95% CI, 0.89-0.99) (unpublished data). Therefore, 1 point
should represent a sufficiently important difference for an individual patient.

The 17-item er-MedDiet score basically captures the 14-items of MEDAS with some additions (sufficiently explained
in our main manuscript) that have been repeatedly associated with benefits in previous observational studies with
good control for confounding. Therefore, a 1-point difference can be also accepted as a minimum clinically important
difference.

For the PDQS (range 0 to 42, SD=3.7), the minimum clinically important difference will represent probably a 2-point
increment, given its wider range.

# Statistical relationship inventory — pass 1 support-B part

Status: PASS_1_COMPLETE. Runtime agent: root/statistics_one_b. Fixed model/effort: gpt-6.1-sol/high; start mode FRESH_SPAWN. This part is owned solely by this fresh statistical pass-1 shard. Pass 2 must use a different newly spawned runtime agent.

## Exact scope and provenance

DOC-004, REPORT: eTable 6 PDF p.16; eTable 7 pp.17–20 (p.20 footnotes included); eTable 8 pp.21–22; eTable 9 pp.23–24; eFigure 3 p.27. There are 10 assigned PDF pages, 61 table variable/population families, 100 between-group time/population contrasts, 300 modeled estimates with 95% CIs (two arm changes and one between-group difference per contrast), 122 baseline arm summaries, 12 figure families with all 48 arm/time distributions, and 4 associated statistical-method/scale records. Stable scope: S401–S477, 77 records. Both time rows and all arms are retained in each applicable family; MI and completer eTable 9 blocks have distinct IDs.

Read QUALITY_CONTROL_SCOPE.md and the entire Workflow 1.6.1 review contract; settings, AGENTS, current routing/tooling, source/evidence inventories, and assigned current mapper views were also read. No old candidate, checker, screening, disposition, classification or final report supplied discovery scope. Sources and reusable assets are preserved.

Evidence locators: current checker_views/results_tables_6_9.md, support_semantic_index.md, results_figures_administrative.md; current main_quantitative_evidence.md used only for matching context. Native layout is the curator's source-matched Poppler pdftotext 26.01.0 extraction of DOC-004. Retained direct-source page images p.16 and p.23 were inspected for the potentially notable score changes and population/test comparison; retained figure_render-27.png was inspected in full. Retained image-engine version is not recorded. No new derivative, OCR, Python, GPU, web access, or source modification was used.

Source links in this part are relative to statistics/parts. Canonical merge into statistics/relationship_inventory.md must shorten their prefix by one directory.

## Shared source-grounded checks and named missing inputs

Each table-family record below includes its complete baseline summaries, both applicable time rows, all three change estimates/intervals, P values, exact source page, population, unit and model context. All displayed change estimates lie within their displayed CIs, and every displayed lower endpoint is no greater than its upper endpoint. Signs of the modeled I-minus-C differences agree with the displayed arm-change directions, allowing each printed quantity its half-unit display interval. Every row received review; none were sampled.

General REPORT methods on DOC-001 p.4 state two-sided tests, P<.05, exploratory secondary analyses with no additional type I error adjustment, mixed-effects linear changes and site/participant/family random intercepts. The assigned table footnotes explicitly state site and couple random factors and 95% CIs. They do not name the statistic used for each P value, its degrees of freedom, exact contrast covariance, variance estimator, or CI construction. eTable 9 does not supply imputation-specific degrees of freedom, pooling formula, or exact variable-by-arm missing counts. These missing definitions preclude exact CI-to-P reconstruction; they are recorded without assumptions. No supplied statistic or standard error occurs in this assigned scope. No numeric normal-theory reconstruction is treated as the reported test.

The tables identify modeled estimates, not crude differences. Differences from subtraction of rounded arm means do not establish a contradiction of the stated models. Baseline-replacement sensitivities, multiple-imputation analyses and completer analyses have different source-established matching keys. No equality of these result families is assumed. Baseline descriptive cross-location comparisons are peripheral; eTable 9 risk-factor contributor counts need not equal nutritional-FFQ counts. No superiority/noninferiority/equivalence margin, interim boundary or stopping conclusion is printed in these assigned tables/figure; the main source calls this interim dietary report preliminary and secondary analyses exploratory. No such decision was inferred.

For every record, PASS_1_COMPLETE denotes completion of checks, including named-input limitations, rather than an adjudication. A future pass-2 reviewer must reconcile the record with the complete ledger/screening/recheck facts and add PASS_2_COMPLETE.

## Associated method, missingness, and unit records

### S474 — Baseline-value replacement sensitivity rule

- **Exact locations and roles:** REPORT DOC-004 [eTable 6 p.16](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=16>), [eTable 7 p.17](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=17>), [eTable 8 p.21](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=21>); REPORT main Statistical Analysis [p.4](<../../../jama_saynorea_2019_oi_190106_1635377898.43062.pdf#page=4>).
- **Printed rule and affected families:** Replace all missing values with baseline; included randomized I3,272/C3,311; 6- and 12-month means (95% CI) fitted with mixed models; all S401–S439. Main source explicitly describes this post hoc sensitivity, distinct from MI and completers.
- **Check / missing definition:** Zero change for an individually replaced value follows the source rule. It does not establish that the fitted marginal mean equals a contributor-weighted crude mean. Model fitting weights and individual missing/observed score changes are unavailable. A direct aggregate scaling of main MI/completer means cannot adjudicate this method.
- **Inclusion outcome / limitations:** No qualifying internally contradictory step; no alternative method proposed. MODELED versus crude observation recorded once in the checker.
- **Pass 1:** PASS_1_COMPLETE.

### S475 — Mixed-effect model and test attribution

- **Exact locations and roles:** REPORT DOC-004 model footnotes [p.16](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=16>), [p.17](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=17>), [p.19](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=19>), [p.20](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=20>), [p.21](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=21>), [p.22](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=22>), [p.24](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=24>); REPORT main Statistical Analysis [p.4](<../../../jama_saynorea_2019_oi_190106_1635377898.43062.pdf#page=4>).
- **Printed statements:** Means (95% CI) calculated using mixed effect models taking into account site and intra-cluster correlations (couples) as random factors. Main source identifies three-level linear model with random intercepts site, participant and family cluster; two-sided tests, P<.05, exploratory secondary analyses and no additional type I error adjustment.
- **Check:** Repeated observations and family/site dependence are explicitly addressed. Abbreviated footnotes do not assert omission of participant dependence. Each table's P applies to its labeled between-group change contrast. There is no source-grounded independent-sample, variance-estimator or covariance contradiction.
- **Precisely missing inputs:** Named per-cell test statistic/test family (e.g. Wald versus likelihood ratio), degrees of freedom, exact covariance matrix/standard errors, CI construction and MI-specific pooling details. Exact estimate–CI–P equality cannot be inferred from a normal approximation. No supplied standard error/test statistic can be crosschecked.
- **Decision rules:** No NI/equivalence margin, multiplicity/interim boundary or stopping conclusion in the assigned displays. No such hypothesis/decision mapping was supplied or inferred.
- **Inclusion outcome:** No emitted finding; missing test-specific details alone screened as CONDITIONAL_OR_UNREPORTED.
- **Pass 1:** PASS_1_COMPLETE.

### S476 — eTable 9 MICE, predictors, fractions, and separate populations

- **Exact locations and roles:** REPORT DOC-004 [eTable 9 p.23](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=23>) and [p.24](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=24>).
- **Printed populations:** MI all included randomized I3,272/C3,311 (sum6,583); completers-only I2,840/C2,946 (sum5,786); 12-month change only. S440–S450 and S451–S461 keep these distinct.
- **Printed method:** Multiple imputation using chained equations; predictors intervention group, sex, age at baseline, smoking status, educational level, physical activity, baseline BMI, total energy intake, and baseline value of variable to be imputed.
- **Every printed imputation fraction:** BMI5%; body weight4.7%; waist9.4%; serum cholesterol10.1%; HDL-c10.8%; LDL-c10.9%; nonHDL-c10.9%; cholesterol:HDL10.9%; triglycerides10.3%; SBP6.1%; DBP6.2%.
- **Check / missing definitions:** Fractions vary by variable, so one overall completer count is not asserted by the source to equal every variable-specific observed count. Exact by-arm observed counts, denominator used for each fraction and common-completer selection rule are not printed. No integer reverse calculation is promoted to a contradiction. Nutrition completer counts concern a different instrument. General main imputation description is not assumed to override this table's explicit chained-equation method.
- **Inclusion outcome:** No emitted finding; unmatched-population and missing-definition observations screened once in the checker.
- **Pass 1:** PASS_1_COMPLETE.

### S477 — Units, conversion factors, and figure standardization

- **Exact locations and roles:** REPORT DOC-004 [eTables 6 p.16](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=16>), [7 p.17](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=17>), [8 p.21](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=21>), [8 p.22](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=22>), [9 p.24](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=24>), [eFigure3 p.27](<../../../joi190106supp3_prod_1635377898.49725.pdf#page=27>).
- **Printed scales and units:** Scores er-MedDiet0–17/MDS0–9/MEDAS0–14/PDQS0–42; foods g/week; energy kcal/d; protein/carbohydrate/fat/SFA/MUFA/PUFA/alcohol %E; MUFA:SFA unitless; fiber and omega-3 g/week; cholesterol mg/week; sodium g/week. Risk units ../../../mg/dL/mmHg or ratio as each family above.
- **Printed conversion factors:** Cholesterol, HDL-c and LDL-c mg/dL ×0.0259 gives mmol/L; triglycerides mg/dL ×0.0113. No corresponding mmol/L result printed for numerical agreement checking.
- **Printed figure definition:** Changes expressed in common units of baseline standard deviations after6 and12months in control/intervention. Labels %kcal and table %E immediately refer to percentage of energy; SD on figure is standardization, not a printed standard error or CI.
- **Check / missing definitions:** Units and directions coherent once energy percentages, raw weekly totals and dimensionless standardized changes are separated. No reported converted pair, exact plotted standardized quantile or arm/pooled-SD denominator is supplied for mechanical recomputation. No missing conversion or preferred transformation is proposed.
- **Inclusion outcome:** No emitted finding; display/scale context and unreported graphical definitions handled under checker exclusions.
- **Pass 1:** PASS_1_COMPLETE.

## Completion and limitations

All 77 S records have PASS_1_COMPLETE. The complete assigned table data, including every time cell/arm, the p.20 continuation footnote, eTable9 fractions/predictors/conversions and all48 eFigure3 distributions, were covered. No scientific source-unit or assigned-family gap. Exact per-cell test statistics, standard errors, degrees of freedom and covariance are unreported; exact graph coordinates/contributor counts and baseline-SD denominator are unreported. These limitations constrain mechanical reproduction but create no finding by themselves. Findings0; consolidated excluded observations8, documented in checkers/parts/statistical_pass_1_support_b.md. No severity, validity, disposition or correction is assigned.

# Statistical pass 2 — individual main and plan relationship statuses

Runtime agent: /root/statistics_two_main. Model: gpt-6.1-sol. Reasoning effort: high. Start mode: FRESH_SPAWN. Distinct from /root/statistics_one_main. Scope: 118 actual IDs S001–S118, comprising 89 main REPORT relationships and 29 PLAN/design families. This unique part does not alter the canonical inventory or any pass-1 artifact.

Each row preserves the actual pass-1 ID and location and supplies an independent second-pass assessment after reading the complete current candidate ledger, inclusion screen and mechanical recheck. Shared definitions: I−C contrast; I3272/C3311 principal nutritional MI cohort; two-sided .05 tests; 95% model intervals; site/participant/family random effects. Exact test, degrees of freedom, standard errors, CI construction, MI pooling and covariance/variance estimator are absent where noted. Normal calculations are diagnostic only. No source absence is filled by convention. S109/S110 are already screened observations and are not new findings. No candidate evidence-state or disposition is assigned.

## Completion and limits

81/81 assigned S records are PASS_2_COMPLETE and retain PASS_1_COMPLETE. Coverage:61 table families,122 inferential time rows,366 point-estimate/CI pairs,122 contrastP values;four complete score-definition families;five shared method/context families;four score panels with24 mean/CI marks and12P annotations;seven favorable-change pairs with14 bars/CI marks and7P annotations. No family,timecell,arm,series,footnote or score-specific count was omitted.

New findings:0. Excluded observation patterns:11 in checkers/parts/statistical_pass_2_support_a.md. Missing exact test/DF/variance/covariance/SE/MI-pooling parameters and exact figure populations/numerators/denominators/CI construction/coordinates constrain numerical reconstruction but create no scientific coverage gap. Geometric values are approximate visual readings,not source-printed coordinates. No candidate classifications, evidence states, human dispositions or corrections are assigned by this statistical part. The coordinator owns the canonical merge.
# Statistical pass 2 — support B relationship statuses

Status: PASS_2_COMPLETE. Fresh runtime task: /root/statistics_two_b; model gpt-6.1-sol; reasoning effort high; start mode FRESH_SPAWN. This runtime is distinct from pass-1 runtime root/statistics_one_b. The coordinator records the runtime ID returned by spawning in agent_execution_manifest.md.

The complete assigned pass-1 inventory was read, followed by an independent revisit of every actual S record below. Current candidate_ledger.md, screening/inclusion_screen.md and verification/evidence_recheck.md were read completely. Their stable-ID set is empty; all three initial findings have SCREENED_OUT decisions. No screened finding is re-emitted. PASS_1_COMPLETE records the existing pass-1 completion; PASS_2_COMPLETE records this independent revisit, including explicitly named mechanical-reproduction limits, without adjudication.

## Assessment conventions

Every table assessment covers both baseline arm summaries and all applicable arm changes, intervals and between-group P values. For eTables 6–8, both 6- and 12-month rows are covered; eTable 9 records each MI and completer family separately at 12 months. In the rows below, a pair of contrasts means 6 months then 12 months, and CI/P agreement means only containment, endpoint order, direction and null-exclusion direction after half-unit rounding. It does not assert exact test reproduction.

The common table population is intervention 3272/control 3311, total 6583. The separate risk-factor completer population is 2840/2946, total 5786. Table means are modeled; descriptive SD/IQR are not standard errors. Exact per-cell statistic family, statistic, degrees of freedom, standard error, contrast covariance, variance estimator, CI construction and MI pooling are absent. No such input is inferred from convention. Figure contributors, exact graphical coordinates and arm-versus-pooled baseline-SD divisor are absent. All four series per figure family are nevertheless inspected.
